# Science Calculators — keyword check (9 Nov 2026)

Checked against the live database (5854 tools, every category). There is no Science main category yet. No target country was given, so tools use US units with a metric option and no country-specific rules are needed (no keyword in the list depends on a jurisdiction).

## A. Summary

| | Count |
|---|---|
| Keywords submitted | 5593 |
| Already built (dropped) | 727 keywords → 128 existing tools |
| Exact duplicates inside the list | 0 |
| Merged (same intent) | 3372 |
| Needs review (not built) | 738 |
| **Unique new tools** | **756** |

Plus **19 existing tools** recommended to move into Science Calculators (section C; not counted as new).

Proposed structure: main category **Science Calculators** with 10 sub-categories (8 of them have 2–5 child sub-categories; no child has fewer than 11 tools, and small groups were folded into broader ones).

## E. Coverage & review notes

**Functionality the kept tools must include (because of merges):**

- `margin-of-error-calculator` (existing) already solves "sample size needed"; rename its H1/title to *Margin of Error & Sample Size Calculator* when it is moved, so the ~20 sample-size keywords land on it.
- `kw-calculator` (existing) absorbs every amps ↔ watts / kVA / hp keyword — keep its DC, single- and three-phase modes.
- `pipe-flow-calculator` (existing) absorbs the generic "flow rate / pipe velocity / gpm" keywords; mass & gas flow get the new Mass & Gas Flow Rate Calculator.
- `beam-load-calculator` (existing) absorbs "shear and moment diagram / bending moment" keywords; the new Beam Deflection Calculator adds cantilever and other support cases.
- `decibel-calculator` and `drag-coefficient-calculator` (existing) cover "decibel distance / inverse square law" and "drag force" respectively.
- Mean, Median, Mode & Range — one data-set input, all four results. Quartile tool shows Q1–Q3, IQR, five-number summary and box-plot fences. Standard Deviation shows sample + population SD and variance.
- Dilution Calculator covers C1V1 = C2V2, molarity dilution, ppm dilution and stock solutions; Molarity covers mg/mL ↔ M. Gas Laws covers Boyle, Charles, Gay-Lussac, Avogadro and combined. Specific Heat covers calorimetry (q = mcΔT).
- Derivative Calculator shows steps for power/product/quotient/chain rules and higher orders; Integral Calculator covers by-parts and trig substitution; Volume of Revolution has disk, washer and shell methods.
- Primer Tm Calculator also returns the annealing temperature (NEB-style rule); Punnett Square handles genotype/phenotype ratios; Hardy-Weinberg returns allele and genotype frequencies.
- Slope Percentage & Gradient (new, general) is kept separate from the existing ski-specific `slope-angle-calculator` and the two-point algebra Slope Calculator.
- Expected Value Calculator (probability distribution) is new because the existing `expected-value-calculator` is betting-only (odds + stake).
- Peptide Reconstitution Calculator is lab dilution maths (vial mg + water mL → concentration and syringe units); it should carry a "not medical advice" note.

**Coverage gaps (recommendations — not part of your list):** the site still has no basic number tools — percentage, fraction, ratio, rounding, square root, average and a general unit converter (length, mass, pressure, energy, temperature). They would fit Math > Algebra & Equations or a future Math/Conversion section.
